{\em kmem\+\_\+buckets\+\_\+create -\/ Create a set of caches that handle dynamic sized allocations via \doxylink{include_2linux_2slab_8h_a14043d12b33ee858117ecb472d4aecfe}{kmem\+\_\+buckets\+\_\+alloc()}

cache. The individual caches with have their sizes as the suffix. @flags:\+ SLAB flags (see \doxylink{include_2linux_2slab_8h_adf8cdc761f51d67846ff22c9b5798a58}{kmem\+\_\+cache\+\_\+create()} for details). @useroffset:\+ Starting offset within an allocation that may be copied to/\+from userspace. @usersize:\+ How many bytes, starting at @useroffset, may be copied to/\+from userspace. @ctor:\+ A constructor for the objects, run when new allocations are made.

Cannot be called within an interrupt, but can be interrupted.

Return:\+ a pointer to the cache on success, NULL on failure. When CONFIG\+\_\+\+SLAB\+\_\+\+BUCKETS is not enabled, ZERO\+\_\+\+SIZE\+\_\+\+PTR is returned, and subsequent calls to \doxylink{include_2linux_2slab_8h_a14043d12b33ee858117ecb472d4aecfe}{kmem\+\_\+buckets\+\_\+alloc()} will fall back to \doxylink{include_2linux_2slab_8h_ab39d1834eaedb0985332521b254f2e01}{kmalloc()}. (i.\+e. callers only need to check for NULL on failure.)

slab\+\_\+folio -\/ The folio allocated for a slab @s:\+ The slab.

Slabs are allocated as folios that contain the individual objects and are using some fields in the first struct page of the folio -\/ those fields are now accessed by struct slab. It is occasionally necessary to convert back to a folio in order to communicate with the rest of the mm. Please use this helper function instead of casting yourself, as the implementation may change in the future.

enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{mm_2slab_8h_add0c1ef8c5770117de419dc9873056f1}{slab\+\_\+state}}}

State of the slab allocator.

This is used to describe the states of the allocator during bootup. Allocators use this to gradually bootstrap themselves. Most allocators have the problem that the structures used for managing slab caches are allocated from slab caches themselves. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}[2]{Enumerator}

Function Description:\+ This function is a wrapper for \+\_\+\+\_\+slab\+\_\+free used specifically by KASAN. It directly frees an object by calling \+\_\+\+\_\+slab\+\_\+free and increments the FREE\+\_\+\+SLOWPATH statistic. {\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{mm_2slab_8h_a594a3dfd377e5dcdd6c1bafe5cc097b4} 
void \+\_\+\+\_\+check\+\_\+heap\+\_\+object (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ptr}{, }\item[{unsigned long}]{n}{, }\item[{const struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structslab}{slab}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{slab}{, }\item[{bool}]{to\+\_\+user}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Rejects incorrectly sized objects and objects that are to be copied to/\+from userspace but do not fall entirely within the containing slab cache\textquotesingle{}s usercopy region.

Returns NULL if check passes, otherwise const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} to name of cache to indicate an error.

Function Description:\+ This function is used by hardened\+\_\+usercopy to check if a user-\/space copy operation is accessing a valid range within a slab object. It verifies the pointer is within the slab, calculates the offset within the object, and checks that the copy falls entirely within the object\textquotesingle{}s usercopy region. If not, it aborts the copy with an error message.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{\_\_kfree\_rcu\_sheaf()}{\_\_kfree\_rcu\_sheaf()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{mm_2slab_8h_abda848a05efa3c522557321b50343d44} 
bool \+\_\+\+\_\+kfree\+\_\+rcu\+\_\+sheaf (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{s}{, }\item[{void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{obj}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Function Description:\+ This function handles deferred freeing of an object via RCU, specifically targeting the percpu sheaf layer. It attempts to place the object into a dedicated rcu\+\_\+free sheaf for the current CPU. If no such sheaf exists, it tries to get an empty one from the spare, the barn, or by allocating a new one. Once the sheaf is full, it is queued for an RCU callback (rcu\+\_\+free\+\_\+sheaf) to be freed later. The function returns true on success, and false if it cannot find or allocate a sheaf. It is not supported on PREEMPT\+\_\+\+RT kernels.

Function Description:\+ This function checks if a \doxylink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache} is completely empty, meaning it has no partial slabs and no slabs in use on any node. {\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{mm_2slab_8h_aa1623a89b7cc8222cf8d8c0dc4c366da} 
int \+\_\+\+\_\+kmem\+\_\+cache\+\_\+shutdown (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{s}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Release all resources used by a slab cache.

Function Description:\+ This is the main function for shutting down a \doxylink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache}. It flushes all per-\/\+CPU caches, waits for any pending RCU sheaves, and then attempts to free all partial slabs on every node. If any slabs are still in use, it returns 1 (indicating the cache is not empty). Otherwise, it returns 0. This function must be called before the cache is destroyed.

Determine object index from a given position

Function Description:\+ Calculates the index of an object within a slab. It subtracts the slab address from the object address and divides by the object size using reciprocal division. This is the internal function for object index calculation. 

Definition at line \doxymbox{\hyperlink{mm_2slab_8h_source_l00336}{336}} of file \doxymbox{\hyperlink{mm_2slab_8h_source}{slab.\+h}}.

Function Description:\+ This function removes the debugfs directory and files associated with a slab cache when the cache is being destroyed. It uses debugfs\+\_\+lookup\+\_\+and\+\_\+remove() to find and remove the directory named after the cache (s-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}name) under the root slab debugfs directory (slab\+\_\+debugfs\+\_\+root). This ensures that debugfs entries are cleaned up properly to prevent stale file references and memory leaks when a cache is unloaded or destroyed.

void defer\+\_\+free\+\_\+barrier (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void}]{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Function Description:\+ This function is a barrier for deferred frees. It synchronously waits for all pending irq\+\_\+work on all CPUs that process deferred objects to complete. This is used to ensure all deferred frees are processed before, for example, a cache is destroyed or a module is unloaded.

Functions provided by the slab allocators

Function Description:\+ Creates a \doxylink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache} with the given parameters. It validates arguments, allocates the cache structure, and registers it. This is the internal function called by the kmem\+\_\+cache\+\_\+create API.

Function Description:\+ This is the main function for creating a new \doxylink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache}. It takes a name, size, and various arguments and flags. It calculates the sizes and order, initializes the per-\/node structures, allocates percpu sheaves, and adds the cache to sysfs. It handles early boot and normal operation. This is called by \doxylink{include_2linux_2slab_8h_adf8cdc761f51d67846ff22c9b5798a58}{kmem\+\_\+cache\+\_\+create()} and other cache creation functions.

void get\+\_\+slabinfo (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{s}{, }\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structslabinfo}{slabinfo}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{sinfo}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Function Description:\+ This function populates a struct slabinfo with statistics for a given \doxylink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache}. It iterates over all NUMA nodes associated with the cache, accumulating the total number of slabs, total objects, and an approximate count of free objects (using \doxylink{slub_8c_ae8bc19bfbec4a86e3bfc27161589f90f}{count\+\_\+partial\+\_\+free\+\_\+approx()} which provides a fast but potentially inexact count). It then calculates active objects as total objects minus free objects. The function also fills in the number of objects per slab and the cache\textquotesingle{}s page order from the cache\textquotesingle{}s oo (order/\+objects) field. This is used by the /\+proc/\+slabinfo interface to provide detailed memory usage statistics for each slab cache.

It initializes the \doxylink{structkmem__cache__node}{kmem\+\_\+cache\+\_\+node} and \doxylink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache} bootstrap caches, creates the kmalloc caches, bootstraps the sheaves for them, sets up freelist randomization, and registers a CPU hotplug handler. This function is called during the kernel\textquotesingle{}s init process.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{mm_2slab_8h_aab67ab42a61b6c5e9839ad29673ab397} 
void kvfree\+\_\+rcu\+\_\+cb (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct rcu\+\_\+head \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{head}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Given an rcu\+\_\+head embedded within an object obtained from kvmalloc at an offset \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} 4k, free the object in question.

Function Description:\+ This is the RCU callback for kvfree\+\_\+rcu(). It determines the type of memory (vmalloc or slab) and frees it appropriately. For vmalloc memory, it calls \doxylink{vmalloc_8h_a2aeead9450a5b920c65bea908edead23}{vfree()}. For slab memory, it finds the object, corrects the pointer to the start of the object (handling red zones), and calls \doxylink{slub_8c_a1561d2946d4fe2250acfb3a28b955c84}{slab\+\_\+free()}.

slub is about to manipulate internal object metadata. This memory lies outside the range of the allocated object, so accessing it would normally be reported by kasan as a bounds error. \doxylink{mm_2slab_8h_a240e1884adee8d995cfcbb2572dcc6cb}{metadata\+\_\+access\+\_\+enable()} is used to tell kasan that these accesses are OK.

Function Description:\+ Enables access to internal slab metadata by disabling KASAN and KMSAN checking. Called before manipulating metadata that would normally be reported as out-\/of-\/bounds.

void setup\+\_\+kmalloc\+\_\+cache\+\_\+index\+\_\+table (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void}]{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

Patch up the size\+\_\+index table if we have strange large alignment requirements for the kmalloc array. This is only the case for MIPS it seems. The standard arches will not generate any code here.

Largest permitted alignment is 256 bytes due to the way we handle the index determination for the smaller caches.

Make sure that nothing crazy happens if someone starts tinkering around with ARCH\+\_\+\+KMALLOC\+\_\+\+MINALIGN

Function Description:\+ Sets up the kmalloc size index table for small allocations. It adjusts the index table based on architecture-\/specific minimum alignment. Called during boot.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{mm_2slab_8h_a4a54ca0cd3dfce7d096274f730d24058} 
unsigned long slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+exts (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structslab}{slab}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{slab}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+exts -\/ get the pointer to the slab object extension vector associated with a slab. @slab:\+ a pointer to the slab struct

Returns the address of the object extension vector associated with the slab, or zero if no such vector has been associated yet. Do not dereference the return value directly; use get/\+put\+\_\+slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+exts() pair and \doxylink{mm_2slab_8h_ae76d33ec8a6836aa17483d459f4faac5}{slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+ext()} to access individual elements.

Example usage:\+

obj\+\_\+exts = slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+exts(slab); if (obj\+\_\+exts) \{ get\+\_\+slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+exts(obj\+\_\+exts); obj\+\_\+ext = slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+ext(slab, obj\+\_\+exts, obj\+\_\+to\+\_\+index(s, slab, obj)); /\+/\+ do something with obj\+\_\+ext put\+\_\+slab\+\_\+obj\+\_\+exts(obj\+\_\+exts); \}

Note that the get/\+put semantics does not involve reference counting. Instead, it updates kasan/\+kmsan depth so that accesses to \doxylink{structslabobj__ext}{slabobj\+\_\+ext} won\textquotesingle{}t be reported as access violations.

Function Description:\+ Returns the object extension vector pointer for a slab. It reads the obj\+\_\+exts field and masks out flag bits. Used to access per-\/object metadata like memcg or allocation profiling.

Function Description:\+ This is the exported function for validating a whole \doxylink{structkmem__cache}{kmem\+\_\+cache}. It flushes all per-\/\+CPU caches, allocates an object map, and then iterates over all nodes, calling \doxylink{slub_8c_ae68b8d14f4bb52be1a1f0532ec946a52}{validate\+\_\+slab\+\_\+node()} for each. It returns the total number of slabs validated, or -\/ENOMEM if the object map could not be allocated. This is a debugging function to check for slab corruption.

Conversion table for small slabs sizes /\+ 8 to the index in the kmalloc array. This is necessary for slabs \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} 192 since we have non power of two cache sizes there. The size of larger slabs can be determined using fls.

Variable Description:\+ A lookup table for small kmalloc sizes. Maps size/\+8 to cache index. Used for fast size-\/to-\/cache lookup for sizes up to 192 bytes. 

Definition at line \doxymbox{\hyperlink{slab__common_8c_source_l00841}{841}} of file \doxymbox{\hyperlink{slab__common_8c_source}{slab\+\_\+common.\+c}}.
